# Astra harness review (wave 2, 2026-09-26) -- dispositions, group by group

The GXemul fork's REGRESSION HARNESS (gate scripts, probes, offline differentials, pipeline checkers at commit `db8b1a33`)
was reviewed read-only by GPT-6 Astra: 722 findings in 18 verification groups (`<job>/codex_<job>.txt`).  Owner
2026-10-02: the working seats are GPT-6 Astra, agy and grok.  Each group below is: the INDEPENDENT seat's verification
and draft disposition of every finding (grok for groups 01-06, 08, 10; agy for 07, 09, 11-18) -> GPT-6 Astra's
confirmation of those DISPOSITIONS (`hconfirm_NN/`; the same model family as the findings, so it rules on the filing,
not on whether its own finding is right) -> the grok seat's first-hand tie-break of the 21 rows where the agy and codex
verifications differed (`adjudication/grok_20261002/grok_htiebreak.txt`).  The Fable and Opus agent seats were
deliberately NOT fired (owner 2026-10-02); a flagship sign-off is batched, not waived.

MECHANICALLY ASSEMBLED (`mk_hdisp.py`, kept beside this file as `mk_hdisp.py.txt`): every line in a group is a verbatim
line, or a verbatim substring of a line, of the file named in its group header.  A draft line gives the key, the
finding's class, the seat's status word and its disposition fields; the seat's explanation is in its own file.
PRECEDENCE: an Astra overturn or amendment governs the draft line it names; a grok tie-break row governs where the two
verifications differed; where a tie-break row and an Astra amendment give one finding different KINDS, both are kept and
the finding returns to the ruling seat (see RESULT; one kind with two grades takes the tie-break's).  Nothing is fixed by this record, and
nothing is filed in `OUTSTANDING_BUGS.md` by it: that is a records round's job.  Proposed ids are the seats' own and are
not yet checked against each other for collisions; the records round does that.

Totals (each from the seats' own VERDICT lines, summed by `mk_hdisp.py`): drafts 717 right, 4 wrong,
1 unsettled of 722; Astra overturned 39 draft dispositions and amended 104 more.
Draft kinds before amendments: (not RIGHT) 5, DROP 14, FILED 163, FILED-BY-PROSE 17, NEW HIGH 18, NEW LOW 230, NEW MED 103, RECORD 172.

Severity for a harness defect is graded by WHAT THE GATE WOULD MISS: HIGH if the defect the check was shipped to catch
ends the emulator process or makes a memory access leave a buffer's bounds; MED if it is guest-visible; LOW otherwise.

ASTRA'S OVERTURNS AND AMENDMENTS (6)
* hbundle_hooks:F1 | draft said NEW MED dashccluster | should be NEW LOW dashccluster | `tools/hooks/wsl_dollar_guard.py:77-78,166-175` confirms the selection gap. The guarded defect is lost shell variables and false harness measurements (`:9-15,68-71`), warranting LOW under your rule. | HIGH confidence
* hbundle_hooks:F2 | draft said NEW MED heredocopener | should be NEW LOW heredocopener | `tools/hooks/wsl_dollar_guard.py:98-106` discards executable text following the heredoc opener and inside executable heredoc bodies. The demonstrated consequence is missed harness protection, not an established guest-visible or host-memory defect. | HIGH confidence
* hbundle_hooks:F3 | draft said NEW MED quoteconcat | should be NEW LOW quoteconcat | `tools/hooks/wsl_dollar_guard.py:123-126` stops at the first closing quote instead of consuming the concatenated argument. The missed shell-variable corruption affects harness execution and records. | HIGH confidence
* hbundle_hooks:F5 | draft said NEW MED dollarbslashnl | should be NEW LOW dollarbslashnl | `tools/hooks/wsl_dollar_guard.py:166-173` splits the continued invocation at its newline. This is another coverage gap in the shell-variable guard; no specific guest/process/buffer defect establishes higher severity. | HIGH confidence
* hbundle_hooks:F7 | draft said NEW LOW nineseatreminder, with RECORD “seven scriptable seats … plus Opus and Fable,” and agy when `PANEL_FIRE_AGY` “is set” | should be NEW LOW nineseatreminder, with corrected historical RECORD: “At db8b1a33, seven default scriptable transports cover six seats: Kimi CLI and kimi_sub cover the same seat. Opus and Fable bring the total to eight. Codex uses gpt-6-astra/max; agy is added when PANEL_FIRE_AGY is nonempty.” Any current operational reminder must follow the owner’s October 2 roster: GPT-6 Astra, agy and grok. | `tools/panel.sh:745-746` explicitly distinguishes transports from seats; `:134,345` requires a nonempty agy setting; `:119,337` establishes Codex configuration. | HIGH confidence
* hbundle_pipeline1:F5 | draft said FILED dashphasedrop, but corrected 173 omitted entries to 168 | should be FILED dashphasedrop; retain **173 entries across 45 rows** | Read-only enumeration of committed `tools/pipeline/ledger.json` gives design 140 + adjudicate 15 + implement 13 + adjudication 3 + records 1 + missing phase 1. The five omitted from the draft’s recount occur at `:24696-24700,46392,47202,47330,47339`. `OUTSTANDING_BUGS.md:15182-15190` already warns that missing phases belong in this denominator. | HIGH confidence

* hbundle_regress2:F2 | OTHER | UNSETTLED: coverage at `:719` is wall time (`total_free >= PREDICT_ON + SLICE_S`) and catch-up warnings are printed, not scored. The 10 Hz product is real: `1048576*115 = 120586240`, times 10 = `1205862400`, and `1205862400 < 2147483647`. Ten is not a source rate. `TIMER_BASE_FREQUENCY` is 65.0 (`src/include/timer.h:33`), `timer_init` stores it (`src/core/timer.c:397`), and the itimer is `(int)(1000000/65) = 15384` usec (`timer.c:327`). Ticks owed per signal at 50e6 Hz are `50000000/65 = 769230.769`, under `1048576` (`timer.h:82`, budget `timer.c:281`), so the cap does not bind and the counter reaches 2^31 at 42.94967296 s, inside the default 23×5 = 115 s. A service rate of at most `2147483647/120586240 ≈ 17.8` Hz would be required for the counter to stay positive for 115 s. A committed count of SIGALRMs serviced during default arm A would settle it. No id until then. | regress/fbpending_witness.py:126-127, regress/fbpending_witness.py:719, src/include/timer.h:33, src/include/timer.h:82, src/core/timer.c:281, src/core/timer.c:327, src/core/timer.c:397
